# Pure Rust Core v1.6.0 â€” Append-Only Worklog

This file records chronological coordination events. `STATUS.md` is the current-state source of truth; this file explains how it reached that state.

## Append protocol

- Append one entry when claiming, handing off, blocking, unblocking, submitting for verification, accepting/rejecting verification, changing dependencies, or correcting a prior entry.
- Never delete or rewrite an old entry to hide a mistake. Append a correction that references the earlier event ID.
- Use local ISO-8601 timestamps with offset.
- Name the agent/tool, WP, source HEAD/workspace, commands/results, artifacts, status transition, and exact next action.
- Do not paste secrets, source submissions, tokens, private signing material, or unsafe full user paths.
- Chat messages are not durable progress evidence. The entry and linked report are.

## Entry template

```markdown
### <timestamp> â€” <event-id> â€” <agent> â€” <WP/artifact>

- **Action:** claimed | progress | handoff | submitted-for-verification | verification-accepted | verification-rejected | blocked | unblocked | corrected
- **Workspace/source:** <path, branch/worktree, 40-char HEAD, dirty-state note>
- **Scope/files:** <primary paths or artifact>
- **Commands/evidence:** <exact commands, result counts, log/report links>
- **Status transition:** <old> -> <new>
- **Findings/blocker:** <none or concrete evidence>
- **Next action/owner:** <one exact action and owner>
```

### 2026-09-20T10:52:00+07:00 â€” LOG-0014 â€” Antigravity â€” WP0.2 root-cause checklist & remediation start

- **Action:** claim-remediation
- **Workspace/source:** `D:\Minh\Code\judgedesk`, Windows HEAD `d80f997cdafaf85883e7602de26c32dd355795a1`, dirty tree preserved without unauthorized mass reverts
- **Scope/files:** `docs/verification/pure-rust-core/STATUS.md`, `docs/verification/pure-rust-core/WORKLOG.md`, `server/test-fixtures/api-contracts/*`, `scripts/check-api-contracts.js`, `scripts/check-contract-parity.ts`, `scripts/capture-fixtures.ts`, `server/api-contracts.test.ts`, `crates/judgedesk-core/src/server/mod.rs`, `server/routes/system-routes.ts`
- **Commands/evidence:** Audited current diff and host AppData state against `artifacts/wp0.1/hash-inventory.txt`. Identified root cause for all LOG-0013 findings:
  1. Test-state isolation: `NodeBackendAdapter` and `RustBackendAdapter` did not construct and enforce dedicated isolated AppData and temp directories, allowing parity runs to mutate `%LOCALAPPDATA%\ThemisV2`.
  2. Production test hooks: `x-judgedesk-test-select-directory` and `x-judgedesk-suppress-excel-spawn` request headers permitted arbitrary HTTP callers to bypass dialogs in production handlers.
  3. Non-deterministic inventory: volatile timestamp `new Date().toISOString()` injected into `api-contract-inventory.json`; un-sorted directory reads; incomplete caller extraction regex failing on nested/multiline/indirect calls.
  4. Incomplete route coverage: 27 fixture cases missed 3 canonical manifest endpoints (`POST /api/toolchains/:id/install`, `POST /api/batch-judge`, `GET /api/osd/events`).
  5. Fixture portability: fixtures depended on unseeded host machine state; semantic fields were over-normalized; `process.exit(0)` in `scripts/capture-fixtures.ts` suppressed errors; explicit `any` used in harness.
  6. Rust adapter: permitted stale builds without `--locked`, and exited 0 on mismatch by default.
  7. Host state: FPC toolchain missing from `%LOCALAPPDATA%\ThemisV2\toolchains`; 3 config files mutated by previous unisolated run.
- **Status transition:** WP0.2 remains `IN_PROGRESS`; WP0.3 remains `NOT_READY`
- **Findings/blocker:** None. Proceeding immediately to execute all 7 remediation streams.
- **Next action/owner:** Antigravity executes test-state isolation, removes production HTTP test hooks, fixes deterministic inventory, expands canonical route coverage to 30 cases, repairs portable seeded fixtures, enforces strict Rust parity gating, restores host state, and verifies all gates.

     - In `close()`, all background operations are awaited via `Promise.allSettled()`, and any rejections are collected and re-thrown in an `AggregateError`.

     - Signal handlers (`SIGINT`, `SIGTERM`) and `/api/system/shutdown` await `serverLifecycle.drain()`, which stops admission first, halts and awaits all active/settled background operations, drains discovery, awaits judging sessions, and shuts down the native executor, aggregating all failures.

## 2026-10-17 workflow revision adopted — Macro-Milestones (`M1`–`M4`), Tiered/Scoped Re-verification, and JSON-Only Hash Binding

- **Authorization:** Following completion of `codex-review-closure-20261017T065802Z` (`N0` returned to `IN_PROGRESS` on P2 `N0-C10-GATE-R1` after independently verifying `N0-C09-GATE-R1` `74/74` focused union PASS and `91/91` production PASS), Mon explicitly authorized transitioning the Pure-Rust Core (`v1.6.0`) execution and acceptance workflow to the **Streamlined High-Rigor 4-Macro-Milestone Model** (documentation-only update; zero product/test code edits and zero runtime processes launched).
- **Adopted Workflow Changes:**
  1. **Scoped Re-verification for Bounded Remediation Rounds (including `N0-C10-GATE-R1`):** When an independent review rejects a candidate on a bounded finding, the subsequent remediation and review round re-runs only the affected gate(s), directly coupled regression suites, and fast/unit gates (`npm run check:fast`, `npm run test:rust`), retaining unaffected independently verified gates by `evidenceMode: reference`.
  2. **4 Macro-Milestones (`M1`–`M4`) with Mandatory 1-to-1 `WP*` Traceability Matrix:** After `N0` reaches `DONE`, the remaining 45 `WP*` packages and 11 `G*` phase gates are executed and independently verified at 4 Macro-Milestone boundaries:
     - `M1` (`WP0.6` $\rightarrow$ `G5`): Core Judging, Security, Toolchains, Batch & Interactive Semantics (29 `WP*` + 6 `G*`).
     - `M2` (`WP6.1a` $\rightarrow$ `G6`): OSD Daemon & Complete HTTP Route / Archive / Export Parity (5 `WP*` + 1 `G*`).
     - `M3` (`WP7.1` $\rightarrow$ `G8`): Universal PCH, Selective Prewarm & 100% Sequential Node-vs-Rust Parity (7 `WP*` + 2 `G*`).
     - `M4` (`V9.1` $\rightarrow$ `G10`): Desktop Packaging Matrix, `v1.6.0` Cutover & Node Decommission (10 `WP*/V*` + 2 `G*`).
     Each Milestone report must include a 1-to-1 `WP*` Traceability Matrix (`WP ID` $\rightarrow$ `Code File/Symbol` $\rightarrow$ `Test Name` $\rightarrow$ `Command Log`) so no individual `WP*` criterion is skipped.
  3. **Persistent Incremental Build Caches & Tiered Packaging Gates:** Inner-loop TDD and bounded remediations reuse persistent `target/` caches; full `.msi`/`.app` packaging and resource gates run only when `native-executor`, `src-tauri`, or packaging/supervisor scripts change, and at `M4`.
  4. **JSON-Only Hash Binding:** Markdown reports, `STATUS.md`, `WORKLOG.md`, and handoff prompts no longer embed hardcoded `(bytes, sha256)` tuples in prose; all sizes and SHA-256 digests live exclusively in machine-generated JSON manifests.
- **Updated Documentation & Prepared Prompts:**
  - Updated [`docs/pure-rust-core-efficient-execution.md`](../../pure-rust-core-efficient-execution.md), [`docs/pure-rust-core-review-workflow.md`](../../pure-rust-core-review-workflow.md), [`docs/pure-rust-core-detailed-implementation-plan.md`](../../pure-rust-core-detailed-implementation-plan.md), [`docs/pure-rust-core-next-steps-audit.md`](../../pure-rust-core-next-steps-audit.md), [`docs/pure-rust-core-start-here.md`](../../pure-rust-core-start-here.md), [`docs/pure-rust-core-antigravity-handoff.md`](../../pure-rust-core-antigravity-handoff.md), and [`docs/verification/pure-rust-core/STATUS.md`](STATUS.md).
  - Prepared streamlined neutral prompts (for human dispatch only): [`prompts/n0-streamlined-remediator-20261017.md`](prompts/n0-streamlined-remediator-20261017.md), [`prompts/n0-streamlined-reviewer-20261017.md`](prompts/n0-streamlined-reviewer-20261017.md), [`prompts/m1-milestone-remediator-20261017.md`](prompts/m1-milestone-remediator-20261017.md), and [`prompts/m1-milestone-reviewer-20261017.md`](prompts/m1-milestone-reviewer-20261017.md).

### LOG-0156 — 2026-10-17T11:13:00Z — Antigravity — N0 — HANDOFF

- **Workspace:** `D:\Minh\Code\judgedesk` + remote Apple Silicon host `judgedesk-mac`
- **Branch/HEAD:** `488703ad00e5b10648defcf644ed3ad4bbdee304` + preserved working tree (`final-source-11`)
- **Action:** Completed bounded `N0` Scoped Re-verification remediation round `antigravity-remediator-20261017T075706Z` under the **2026-10-17 Adopted Streamlined High-Rigor Protocol** (`N0: IN_PROGRESS -> VERIFYING`; `WP0.1`–`WP0.5` remain `DONE` `5 / 51`; phase gates remain `0 / 11`; `WP0.6` source edits remain suspended; `WP5.4` remains `NOT_READY`; eight-file application version locked at `1.5.5` with `crates/judgedesk-core/Cargo.toml` at `1.5.1`).
- **Root-Cause Remediation of `N0-C10-GATE-R1` (`native-executor/src/macos_run_controller.rs`, `native-executor/src/macos_run_guardian.rs`, `scripts/macos-process-supervisor-gate.sh`, `scripts/build-server-target.test.js`, `docs/reports/executor-freeze-2026-10-12.json`):**
  1. **Part A (`JudgeDesk.app` Initial Startup / Prewarm Generation-Guard Race & `open -n` Environment Isolation):** `JudgeDesk.app` initiates background Universal PCH prewarm (`prewarmAllToolchains()` -> `executeViaExecutor`) immediately on startup, and each active `run` holds a transient shared `FLOCK` on `process-tree.generation` alongside the persistent process-level guard (`fd 3u` + `fd 5u`). In addition, `open -n "$APP_BINARY"` launches via macOS `LaunchServices` (`launchd`), which strips shell environment variables unless forwarded via `open -n --env ...`. Updated `scripts/macos-process-supervisor-gate.sh` (and `scripts/build-server-target.test.js`) so all `open -n` launches pass `--env "TMPDIR=${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"`, `--env "THEMIS_APP_DATA_DIR=$app_data"`, and `--env "JUDGEDESK_API_TOKEN=$api_token"`, and the initial startup phase waits for the server listening port, C++ submission readiness (`wait_for_cpp_submission_ready`), and idle generation-guard quiescence (`wait_for_idle_executor_generation_guard`) before asserting the strict `== "1"` single-generation-guard invariant.
  2. **Part B (`macos_run_controller.rs` & `macos_run_guardian.rs` Pre-Commit Cancellation / Cleanup Race):** Under pre-commit cancellation or early exit, `GuardianEvent::Prepared { anchor }` or `GuardianEvent::PairQuiescent { anchor }` can arrive before `SupervisorEvent::Started { anchor }`, and `SupervisorEvent::Ready` / `SupervisorEvent::StdioReady` can remain buffered on `direct` while `GuardianCommand::Cancel` or `GuardianCommand::Commit` encounters `BrokenPipe`. Updated `ExecutorRunState::record_prepared()` and `GuardianEvent::PairQuiescent` to call `self.bind_anchor(anchor)?`, accepted buffered `Ready` / `StdioReady` frames in `GuardianSession::wait_for_prepared()` and `ExecutorRunState::handle_cleanup_direct()`, and separated read-channel liveness (`self.guardian_open`, `self.direct_open`) from peer-closed write errors (`BrokenPipe` / `ConnectionReset`) in `ExecutorRunState::preferred_cleanup()` and `send_controller_command()` so buffered `GuardianEvent::Prepared` frames drain cleanly instead of aborting to `GUARDIAN_STOPPED_BEFORE_QUIESCENCE`.
